# Lane F — food-allocation comparison: generated tables

Generated by `Proto.M7.Econ food-compare` from REAL Sim.Core baselines (`food-baseline`). 10128 battery runs + 24 validation runs in 68492 ms. CSV SHA-256 `47b67aebef3224d6cc5e1787bb04f622f50ce8f941fdb438b95500456e97cf0c`.

Orders: **CGM** = civilian → growth → military; **CMG** = civilian → military → growth. Neither is adopted (D-079 Q3).

Shipped constants read: spoilage 0.080/yr, granary 1.50 y of demand, upkeep 1.00/person-yr, starvation max 0.120/yr, absorbable shortfall 0.20, fertility suppression slope 3.0, headroom k 0.0693/yr, sigmaLogYield 0.2936 (regime multipliers e^-1s 0.7456, e^-2s 0.5559), window = correlationTimeYears 3.0 y; march: 40 km/day, 16 km/cost unit, haul 0.16 per bulk·cost unit.

## 8. Qualitative divergence between the orders (pairs identical except the order; counts of seeds)

Columns: **pairs** = seeds with both runs; **ident** = byte-identical final state; **ration** = one order feeds the army in full and the other does not; **growth** = one serves the growth claim in full and the other does not; **civ starve** = civilian starvation (vs PEACE) > 0 under one order only; **soldiers** = different army size at the window's end; **pop +20 / +100 / +300: CGM>CMG** = seeds where CGM ends with more people; **crossover** = the sign of (CGM − CMG) population flips between +20 and the last checkpoint.

## 12. Fate of the overflow: TRADED (TradeCredit, TradeCreditSinceFounding: PROPOSALS, bounding) against LOST (CapBeforeMilitary, existing) — same timing, per War Pulse, FULL haul, mean over seeds

TradeCredit sells the grain above the capacity at the numeraire price 1 (D-033) for a credit and buys it back at the same price, with no transport cost, whenever the store cannot cover civilians or a granted claim. Its credit opens at 0 at T0. TradeCreditSinceFounding opens it at the grain the REAL world destroyed as GranaryOverflow in turns 1..T0 (an upper estimate). Credit at end = opening + Σ exported − Σ imported.

## 13. Where the army's grain came from under TradeCredit: bought back, or released from the physical store (verification round 1) — consequence None, FULL haul, mean over seeds

Trade CSV SHA-256 `6946b581870fa06307b19269aa4531556240b482d38cf6a64e986b8c1dda272b` (one row per trade-rule run; per-seed values of every trade column).

The allocator under TradeCredit counts store + credit. The growth-headroom grant is not a physical draw, so it can be granted against the credit while the physical store is released to the army: an accounting effect that needs no import. Draws = ration + haul granted (growth is headroom here). Bought back = imports for draws (imports for civilians are shown separately). From the store = draws − bought back. Span: the whole horizon per campaign turn, the war window per War Pulse.
